📚 本期文献进展 / Literature Brief

  • 本期埃博拉病毒类群研究集中呈现以下进展。
  • 重组 BDBV-ZsG 表现出与野生型 BDBV 亲本毒株相当的抑制特性,并在单克隆抗体和恢复期血清样品的中和测定中显示出强大的灵敏度和特异性。 [10.1080/22221751.2026.2709847]。
  • 历史病死率已从 1970 年代的约 65% 下降至最近的 暴发 的约 50%,预计较 疫苗 的影响减少 15%;然而,目前还没有获得许可的 疫苗 或针对本迪布焦埃博拉病毒的靶向治疗药物。
  • 比较分析确定了分布在整个 GP 序列中的 21 个核心氨基酸取代位点。 [10.21203/rs.3.rs-10265918/v1]。
  • 菌血症在 CCHF 患者中最常见 (n = 46),其次是 EVD (n = 16)、MVD (n = 16) 和 LF (n = 2)。在非洲以外地区接受治疗的 27 名埃博拉病毒病患者中,有 7 名 (26%) 患有细菌合并感染。在 HCVI 中,菌血症在症状出现后的中位时间(范围 0-23)检测到 11 天,并且与败血症的特征相关。
  • This reporting window highlights the following research developments.
  • The recombinant BDBV-ZsG demonstrated comparable inhibitory profiles to the wild-type BDBV parental strain and showed robust sensitivity and specificity in neutralization assays with monoclonal antibodies and convalescent serum samples.
  • Historical case fatality rates have declined from approximately 65% in the 1970s to around 50% in recent outbreaks, with an estimated 15% reduction from vaccine impact; however, no licensed vaccines or targeted therapeutics exist for Bundibugyo ebolavirus.
  • Comparative analysis identified 21 core amino acid substitution sites distributed throughout the GP sequence. [10.21203/rs.3.rs-10265918/v1].
  • Bacteremia was most commonly reported among patients with CCHF (n = 46), followed by EVD (n = 16), MVD (n = 16), and LF (n = 2). Of 27 EVD patients treated outside Africa, seven (26%) had bacterial coinfection.

📘 主报告：研究论文 / Primary Research

Journal: Emerging microbes & infections  |  Canonical publication date: 2026-07-24  |  DOI: 10.1080/22221751.2026.2709847  |  Date basis: first_publication_date  |  Publication status: Current window  |  Citation: 2026, 2709847
A 高优先级学术文献 · 研究A high priorityAcademic literature · Research

表达荧光报告蛋白的重组 Bundibugyo 病毒的开发和表征。

Development and Characterization of a Recombinant Bundibugyo Virus Expressing a Fluorescent Reporter Protein.
作者： Shilpi Jain, Éric Bergeron, Mike Flint, Joel M. Montgomery, Christina F. Spiropoulou, César G. Albariño, Bergeron É
摘要中文翻译摘要 由本迪布焦病毒 (BDBV) 引起的本迪布焦病毒病 (BVD) 具有较高的发病率和 死亡率 相关性,此前 暴发 报告的病死率为 30%-50%。刚果民主共和国和乌干达正在进行的 BDBV 暴发 凸显了对病毒特异性研究工具和医疗对策的迫切需要。与扎伊尔埃博拉病毒引起的 埃博拉病毒病 不同,目前尚无获得许可的 疫苗 或特定疗法可用于 BVD。研究工具的缺乏限制了 BDBV 的研究。为了促进 抗病毒药物 测试和中和研究,我们开发了第一个表达荧光报告蛋白 ZsGreen (rBDBV-ZsG) 的重组 BDBV。作为概念证明,我们测试了一组先前表征的 抗病毒药物 化合物,并证明了 rBDBV-ZsG 和野生型 BDBV 亲本毒株之间具有相当的抑制特性。此外,rBDBV-ZsG 的实用性在中和测定中得到了成功评估,证明了使用单克隆抗体和恢复期血清样本的强大敏感性和特异性。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本迪布焦病毒病 (BVD) 导致较高的发病率,死亡率 的病死率为 30%-50%,而正在进行的 暴发 突显了对病毒特异性研究工具和医疗对策的迫切需要,因为目前没有针对 BVD 的许可 疫苗 或特定疗法。
设计与对象
该研究开发了第一个表达荧光报告蛋白 ZsGreen (rBDBV-ZsG) 的重组本迪布焦病毒,以促进 抗病毒药物 测试和中和研究。
核心方法
使用先前表征的 抗病毒药物 化合物对重组病毒进行了测试,以将抑制谱与野生型 BDBV 亲本毒株进行比较,并使用单克隆抗体和恢复期血清样品在中和测定中评估其效用。
主要结果
重组 BDBV-ZsG 表现出与野生型 BDBV 亲本毒株相当的抑制特性,并在单克隆抗体和恢复期血清样品的中和测定中显示出强大的灵敏度和特异性。
解释与创新
rBDBV-ZsG的开发为抗病毒药物测试和中和研究提供了宝贵的工具,解决了之前缺乏BDBV研究工具的局限性。
科研与公卫意义
该重组病毒可以通过有效筛选 抗病毒药物 化合物和评估 免疫应答 来加速 BVD 医学对策的开发。
局限与证据强度
该研究仅限于体外概念验证测试,但由于与野生型 BDBV 的直接比较以及多次测定的成功验证,证据强度很高。

Development and Characterization of a Recombinant Bundibugyo Virus Expressing a Fluorescent Reporter Protein.

Authors: Shilpi Jain, Éric Bergeron, Mike Flint, Joel M. Montgomery, Christina F. Spiropoulou, César G. Albariño, Bergeron É
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Bundibugyo virus disease (BVD), caused by Bundibugyo virus (BDBV), is associated with substantial morbidity and mortality, with previous outbreaks reporting case fatality rates of 30%-50%. The ongoing BDBV outbreak in the Democratic Republic of the Congo and Uganda highlights the urgent need for virus-specific research tools and medical countermeasures. Unlike Ebola virus disease caused by Zaire ebolavirus, no licensed vaccines or specific therapeutics are currently available for BVD. The lack of research tools has limited studies with BDBV. To facilitate antiviral testing and neutralization studies, we developed the first recombinant BDBV expressing the fluorescent reporter protein ZsGreen (rBDBV-ZsG). As a proof of concept, we tested a set of previously characterized antiviral compounds and demonstrated comparable inhibitory profiles between rBDBV-ZsG and the wild-type BDBV parental strain. Furthermore, the utility of rBDBV-ZsG was successfully evaluated in neutralization assays, demonstrating robust sensitivity and specificity using monoclonal antibodies and convalescent serum samples.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Bundibugyo virus disease (BVD) causes substantial morbidity and mortality with case fatality rates of 30%-50%, and the ongoing outbreak highlights the urgent need for virus-specific research tools and medical countermeasures, as no licensed vaccines or specific therapeutics are currently available for BVD.
Design and population
The study developed the first recombinant Bundibugyo virus expressing the fluorescent reporter protein ZsGreen (rBDBV-ZsG) to facilitate antiviral testing and neutralization studies.
Core methods
The recombinant virus was tested with previously characterized antiviral compounds to compare inhibitory profiles with the wild-type BDBV parental strain, and its utility was evaluated in neutralization assays using monoclonal antibodies and convalescent serum samples.
Main results
The recombinant BDBV-ZsG demonstrated comparable inhibitory profiles to the wild-type BDBV parental strain and showed robust sensitivity and specificity in neutralization assays with monoclonal antibodies and convalescent serum samples.
Interpretation and novelty
The development of rBDBV-ZsG provides a valuable tool for antiviral testing and neutralization studies, addressing the previous limitation of lacking research tools for BDBV.
Scientific and public-health significance
The recombinant virus can accelerate the development of medical countermeasures for BVD by enabling efficient screening of antiviral compounds and evaluation of immune responses.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited to in vitro proof-of-concept testing, but the evidence strength is high due to the direct comparison with wild-type BDBV and successful validation in multiple assays.

埃博拉病毒病、马尔堡病毒病、克里米亚-刚果出血热和拉沙热的细菌感染

Bacterial infection in Ebola virus disease, Marburg virus disease, Crimean–Congo hemorrhagic fever, and Lassa fever
作者： Brady Page, Elizabeth Moreton, David A. Wohl, William A. Fischer
摘要中文翻译摘要 背景 由于对高后果病毒感染 (HCVI) 患者细菌合并感染的担忧,经验性抗生素给药已成为护理标准。然而,HCVI 中细菌共感染的 发病率、微生物学和临床意义仍不清楚。方法 我们对 病例报告、队列研究 研究以及描述 埃博拉病毒病 (EVD)、马尔堡病毒病 (MVD)、克里米亚-刚果出血热 (CCHF) 和拉沙热 (LF) 细菌共感染的横断面分析进行了系统回顾。提取了有关研究设计、设置、诊断方法、病原体、抗生素使用和结果的数据。结果 30 篇出版物符合纳入标准。菌血症在 CCHF 患者中最常见 (n = 46),其次是 EVD (n = 16)、MVD (n = 16) 和 LF (n = 2)。在非洲以外地区接受治疗的 27 名埃博拉病毒病患者中,有 7 名 (26%) 患有细菌合并感染。在 HCVI 中,菌血症在症状出现后的中位时间(范围 0-23)检测到 11 天,并且与败血症的特征相关。病原体包括肠道菌群、医疗保健相关微生物、机会性病原体和人畜共患细菌,尤其是布鲁氏菌属。头孢曲松是最常见的经验性抗生素,但预计对少数血流分离株具有活性。EVD 或 MVD 合并感染患者的 死亡率 与未合并细菌感染的患者相似,而合并 CCHF 患者的 死亡率 高于未合并感染的患者(15.5%;95% CI 8.0–25.9% vs 5.7%;95% CI 5.1–6.4%)。结论 尽管是推动经验性抗生素使用的一个主要问题,但 HCVI 中的细菌合并感染仍然缺乏描述,无法确定其 发病率 或临床意义。需要对细菌 诊断 的标准化方案和抗菌药物耐药性特征进行前瞻性研究,以指导抗菌策略。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
对严重后果病毒感染 (HCVI) 患者细菌合并感染的担忧已使经验性抗生素给药成为护理标准,但 HCVI 中细菌合并感染的 发病率、微生物学和临床意义仍不清楚。
设计与对象
我们对 病例报告、队列研究 研究以及描述 埃博拉病毒病 (EVD)、马尔堡病毒病 (MVD)、克里米亚-刚果出血热 (CCHF) 和拉沙热 (LF) 细菌共感染的横断面分析进行了系统回顾。
核心方法
从符合纳入标准的 30 篇出版物中提取了有关研究设计、设置、诊断方法、病原体、抗生素使用和结果的数据。
主要结果
菌血症在 CCHF 患者中最常见 (n = 46),其次是 EVD (n = 16)、MVD (n = 16) 和 LF (n = 2)。在非洲以外地区接受治疗的 27 名埃博拉病毒病患者中,有 7 名 (26%) 患有细菌合并感染。在 HCVI 中,菌血症在症状出现后的中位时间(范围 0-23)检测到 11 天,并且与败血症的特征相关。
解释与创新
尽管是推动经验性抗生素使用的一个主要问题,但 HCVI 中的细菌合并感染仍然缺乏描述,无法确定其 发病率 或临床意义。
科研与公卫意义
需要对细菌 诊断 的标准化方案和抗菌药物耐药性特征进行前瞻性研究,以指导抗菌策略。
局限与证据强度
由于依赖于 病例报告 和 队列研究 研究,而细菌 诊断 和抗菌素耐药性表征的标准化方案有限,因此证据强度中等。

Bacterial infection in Ebola virus disease, Marburg virus disease, Crimean–Congo hemorrhagic fever, and Lassa fever

Authors: Brady Page, Elizabeth Moreton, David A. Wohl, William A. Fischer
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Background Concern for bacterial coinfection in patients with high consequence viral infections (HCVIs) has made the empiric antibiotic administration the standard of care. However, the incidence, microbiology, and clinical significance of bacterial coinfection in HCVIs remain unclear. Methods We conducted a systematic review of case reports, cohort studies, and cross-sectional analyses describing bacterial coinfection in Ebola virus disease (EVD), Marburg virus disease (MVD), Crimean–Congo hemorrhagic fever (CCHF), and Lassa fever (LF). Data were extracted on study design, setting, diagnostic methods, pathogens, antibiotic use, and outcomes. Results Thirty publications met inclusion criteria. Bacteremia was most commonly reported among patients with CCHF (n = 46), followed by EVD (n = 16), MVD (n = 16), and LF (n = 2). Of 27 EVD patients treated outside Africa, seven (26%) had bacterial coinfection. Across HCVIs, bacteremia was detected a median of 11 days after symptom onset (range 0–23) and was associated with features of sepsis. Pathogens included enteric flora, healthcare-associated organisms, opportunistic pathogens, and zoonotic bacteria—most notably Brucella spp. Ceftriaxone was the most common empiric antibiotic but is predicted to have activity against a minority of bloodstream isolates. Mortality among coinfected patients with EVD or MVD was similar to those without bacterial coinfection, whereas coinfected CCHF patients had higher mortality than those without coinfection (15.5%; 95% CI 8.0–25.9% vs 5.7%; 95% CI 5.1–6.4%). Conclusions Despite being a major concern driving empiric antibiotic use, bacterial coinfection in HCVIs remains poorly described, precluding determination of its incidence or clinical significance. Prospective studies with standardized protocols for bacterial diagnosis and characterization of antimicrobial resistance are needed to guide antimicrobial strategies.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Concern for bacterial coinfection in patients with high consequence viral infections (HCVIs) has made empiric antibiotic administration standard of care, yet the incidence, microbiology, and clinical significance of bacterial coinfection in HCVIs remain unclear.
Design and population
We conducted a systematic review of case reports, cohort studies, and cross-sectional analyses describing bacterial coinfection in Ebola virus disease (EVD), Marburg virus disease (MVD), Crimean–Congo hemorrhagic fever (CCHF), and Lassa fever (LF).
Core methods
Data were extracted on study design, setting, diagnostic methods, pathogens, antibiotic use, and outcomes from thirty publications that met inclusion criteria.
Main results
Bacteremia was most commonly reported among patients with CCHF (n = 46), followed by EVD (n = 16), MVD (n = 16), and LF (n = 2). Of 27 EVD patients treated outside Africa, seven (26%) had bacterial coinfection. Across HCVIs, bacteremia was detected a median of 11 days after symptom onset (range 0–23) and was associated with features of sepsis.
Interpretation and novelty
Despite being a major concern driving empiric antibiotic use, bacterial coinfection in HCVIs remains poorly described, precluding determination of its incidence or clinical significance.
Scientific and public-health significance
Prospective studies with standardized protocols for bacterial diagnosis and characterization of antimicrobial resistance are needed to guide antimicrobial strategies.
Limitations and evidence strength
The evidence strength is moderate due to the reliance on case reports and cohort studies with limited standardized protocols for bacterial diagnosis and characterization of antimicrobial resistance.

疫苗 本迪布焦埃博拉病毒 暴发 控制方面的差距及其对全球公共卫生准备和应对的影响

Vaccine gaps in Bundibugyo ebolavirus outbreak control and their implications for global public health preparedness and response
作者： Frank Adusei-Mensah
摘要中文翻译摘要:本研究旨在严格检查复发性 埃博拉病毒病 (EVD) 暴发,特别是本迪布焦埃博拉病毒,重点关注流行病学趋势以及​​疫苗学和公共卫生准备方面的差距,以便为协调一致的 暴发 应对措施和全球卫生安全战略提供信息。历史趋势显示,总体病死率从 1970 年代的约 65% 逐渐下降到最近的 暴发 的约 50%,反映出临床护理、监测 和响应系统的改进以及潜在的 疫苗 影响,根据 ITS 分析,估计 CFR 降低约 15%。然而,高收入和低资源环境之间仍然存在巨大差距。当前的疫情暴露了生物医学准备方面的重大差距,因为与扎伊尔埃博拉病毒不同,本迪布焦埃博拉病毒不存在获得许可的 疫苗 或靶向治疗药物。因此,暴发 控制继续严重依赖非药物干预措施,包括病例检测、隔离、接触者追踪和社区参与。冲突、人口流离失所和薄弱的卫生基础设施加剧了持续的 传播。加强准备工作需要对 疫苗 开发进行持续的全球投资,改进 监测 人与动物界面以及协调的国际研究和响应框架。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在严格检查复发性 埃博拉病毒病 暴发,特别是本迪布焦埃博拉病毒,重点关注流行病学趋势以及​​疫苗学和公共卫生准备方面的差距,为 暴发 应对和全球卫生安全战略提供信息。
设计与对象
该研究分析了 埃博拉病毒病 病死率的历史趋势,并研究了高收入和低资源环境之间的差异,重点关注本迪布焦埃博拉病毒 暴发。
核心方法
该研究采用中断时间序列分析来估计 疫苗 对病死率的影响,并检查当前流行病的生物医学准备差距,特别是本迪布焦埃博拉病毒。
主要结果
历史病死率已从 1970 年代的约 65% 下降至最近的 暴发 的约 50%,预计较 疫苗 的影响减少 15%;然而,目前还没有获得许可的 疫苗 或针对本迪布焦埃博拉病毒的靶向治疗药物。
解释与创新
研究结果强调,与扎伊尔埃博拉病毒不同,由于本迪布焦埃博拉病毒的生物医学准备工作存在严重差距,暴发 的控制仍然严重依赖非药物干预措施。
科研与公卫意义
加强准备工作需要全球对疫苗的发展进行持续投资,改进人与动物接触方面的监测,并协调国际研究和响应框架,以解决因冲突和卫生基础设施薄弱而加剧的持续传播问题。
局限与证据强度
证据强度中等,因为该研究依赖于历史趋势分析并强调了关键差距,但没有提供新的主要数据或随机对照试验。

Vaccine gaps in Bundibugyo ebolavirus outbreak control and their implications for global public health preparedness and response

Authors: Frank Adusei-Mensah
Original abstract (English)
Abstract The present study aims to critically examine recurrent Ebola virus disease (EVD) outbreaks—particularly Bundibugyo ebolavirus—with a focus on epidemiological trends, and gaps in vaccinology and public health preparedness, in order to inform concerted outbreak response and global health security strategies. Historical trends show a gradual decline in overall case fatality rates from approximately 65% in the 1970s to around 50% in recent outbreaks, reflecting improvements in clinical care, surveillance, and response systems and potential vaccine impact with estimated approximately 15% impact in CFR reduction from the ITS analysis. However, substantial disparities remain between high-income and low-resource settings. The current epidemic exposes a critical gap in biomedical preparedness, as no licensed vaccines or targeted therapeutics exist for Bundibugyo ebolavirus, unlike Zaire ebolavirus. Hence, outbreak control continues to rely heavily on non-pharmaceutical interventions, including case detection, isolation, contact tracing, and community engagement. Ongoing transmission is exacerbated by conflict, population displacement, and weak health infrastructure. Strengthening preparedness will require sustained global investment in vaccine development, improved surveillance at human–animal interfaces and coordinated international research and response frameworks.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aims to critically examine recurrent Ebola virus disease outbreaks, particularly Bundibugyo ebolavirus, focusing on epidemiological trends and gaps in vaccinology and public health preparedness to inform outbreak response and global health security strategies.
Design and population
The study analyzes historical trends in Ebola virus disease case fatality rates and examines disparities between high-income and low-resource settings, with a focus on Bundibugyo ebolavirus outbreaks.
Core methods
The study employs an interrupted time series analysis to estimate the impact of vaccines on case fatality rates and examines the current epidemic's biomedical preparedness gaps, particularly for Bundibugyo ebolavirus.
Main results
Historical case fatality rates have declined from approximately 65% in the 1970s to around 50% in recent outbreaks, with an estimated 15% reduction from vaccine impact; however, no licensed vaccines or targeted therapeutics exist for Bundibugyo ebolavirus.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings highlight that outbreak control continues to rely heavily on non-pharmaceutical interventions due to the critical gap in biomedical preparedness for Bundibugyo ebolavirus, unlike Zaire ebolavirus.
Scientific and public-health significance
Strengthening preparedness requires sustained global investment in vaccine development, improved surveillance at human-animal interfaces, and coordinated international research and response frameworks to address ongoing transmission exacerbated by conflict and weak health infrastructure.
Limitations and evidence strength
The evidence strength is moderate as the study relies on historical trend analysis and highlights critical gaps but does not present new primary data or randomized controlled trials.

本迪布焦病毒 暴发:当相关病原体遇到人道主义紧急情况时。

Bundibugyo virus outbreak: when a concerning pathogen meets a humanitarian emergency.

由于布尼亚省级实验室的埃博拉病毒检测呈阴性,实验室确认被推迟,需要将标本送往金沙萨进行物种特异性鉴定。

Bundibugyo virus outbreak: when a concerning pathogen meets a humanitarian emergency.

Authors: Nahid Bhadelia, Bhadelia N.
Original abstract (English)
The Democratic Republic of the Congo has declared its seventeenth Ebola outbreak, caused by the Bundibugyo virus, which had been circulating undetected for months. The response is now catching up across a region weakened by conflict, displacement and loss of humanitarian aid. With no licensed vaccine or treatment, containment will depend on rebuilding resources, access and trust in public health systems. The Democratic Republic of the Congo (DRC) and its neighbouring countries face an incredible challenge in the new Bundibugyo virus (BDBV) disease outbreak. By the time the Institut National de Recherche Biomédicale in Kinshasa confirmed BDBV in Ituri Province, officials had already recorded 246 suspected cases and 80 suspected deaths across several health zones. Confirmed cases were soon logged across multiple DRC provinces and in Kampala, the capital of neighbouring Uganda1. The WHO declared a Public Health Emergency of International Concern on 17 May, and the Africa Centres for Disease Control and Prevention followed with a Public Health Emergency of Continental Security on 18 May, naming ten other countries at high risk of spread2,3. The scale of those initial figures made it evident that the world was not discovering a new outbreak so much as uncovering one that had been silently circulating, hidden by the fragility of local healthcare capacity and propagated by conflict, community distrust and population mobility. As of 31 May, 336 cases had been confirmed across both countries, with 49 confirmed deaths, alongside 220 suspected cases and 236 suspected deaths in the DRC. These numbers are almost certainly an undercount. A modelling group at Imperial College London estimated within days of the declaration that true infections probably already exceeded 900, and many...
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study addresses the challenges of the Bundibugyo virus outbreak in the Democratic Republic of the Congo and neighbouring countries, highlighting the public health crisis caused by a pathogen that had been circulating undetected for months.
Design and population
The outbreak was documented across multiple provinces in the DRC and in Kampala, Uganda, with confirmed cases and deaths recorded as of 31 May.
Core methods
Laboratory confirmation was delayed due to negative Ebola testing at the provincial laboratory in Bunia, requiring specimens to be sent to Kinshasa for species-specific identification.
Main results
As of 31 May, 336 cases had been confirmed across both countries, with 49 confirmed deaths, alongside 220 suspected cases and 236 suspected deaths in the DRC.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpret the outbreak as a consequence of delayed laboratory identification and regional healthcare fragility, exacerbated by conflict and population mobility, making it one of the largest Ebola crises.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings underscore the need for rebuilding public health resources, access, and trust, as containment depends on these factors in the absence of licensed vaccines or treatments.
Limitations and evidence strength
The evidence is limited by undercounting of cases and deaths, with modelling suggesting true infections may exceed 900, and the strength is moderate due to reliance on observational data and retrospective analysis.

埃博拉病毒 持久性和免疫特权的隐藏成本。

Ebola virus persistence and the hidden cost of immune privilege.
作者： Amal Fahmi, Isabel Roxana Schultz-Pernice, Marco P. Alves, Isabel Schultz-Pernice, Marco P Alves, Fahmi A, Schultz-Pernice I, Alves MP.
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在了解 埃博拉病毒 如何利用中枢神经系统等免疫特权环境来建立持续感染,解决病毒持续机制的知识空白以及免疫特权在慢性感染中的作用。
设计与对象
该研究使用人脑类器官来模拟中枢神经系统环境,检查多种神经细胞类型(包括神经元、星形胶质细胞、少突胶质细胞和小胶质细胞)的 埃博拉病毒 感染动态。
核心方法
该研究描述了脑类器官中病毒复制动力学、传播机制、病毒进化和宿主 免疫应答的特征,重点关注病毒通过出芽和细胞间传播的传播,并分析有缺陷的病毒基因组和炎症介质的产生。
主要结果
埃博拉病毒 感染人脑类器官中的多种神经细胞类型,建立与病毒 进化 相关的持续感染、缺陷病毒 基因组 的积累以及激活的小胶质细胞产生促炎介质和 I 型干扰素。
解释与创新
作者解释说,免疫特权通过限制病原体清除同时允许慢性低水平复制来实现 埃博拉病毒 的持续存在,新的发现是这种持续存在通过病毒 进化 和免疫激活导致慢性神经炎症和神经元损伤。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现为监测在中枢神经系统中持续存在的策略提供了信息,强调了在免疫特权部位针对病毒储存宿主的治疗的需要,并提示幸存者可能出现神经系统并发症。
局限与证据强度
尽管该研究提供了 埃博拉病毒 在神经组织中持久性的直接机制见解,但由于使用的类器官模型可能无法完全复制体内的复杂性,因此证据强度中等。

Ebola virus persistence and the hidden cost of immune privilege.

Authors: Amal Fahmi, Isabel Roxana Schultz-Pernice, Marco P. Alves, Isabel Schultz-Pernice, Marco P Alves, Fahmi A, Schultz-Pernice I, Alves MP.

As a result, immune-privileged niches may be co-opted by pathogens as long-term reservoirs and enable persistence despite systemic immune surveillance. Alves Human brain organoids reveal how Ebola virus establishes persistent infection within the immune-privileged environment of the central nervous system. The idea of immune privilege has reshaped our understanding of how the immune system is regulated across tissues. It is viewed as a state of selective immune tolerance characterized by tissue- and organ-specific immune regulation. Classic examples include the central nervous sys- tem (CNS), eyes and testes, where immune surveillance is present but constrained. These sites maintain a highly regulated immunological milieu through cellular barriers, local immunoregulatory mediators and context-dependent modulation of antigen presentation. View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to understand how Ebola virus exploits immune-privileged environments like the central nervous system to establish persistent infection, addressing the knowledge gap in viral persistence mechanisms and the role of immune privilege in chronic infections.
Design and population
The research used human brain organoids to model the central nervous system environment, examining Ebola virus infection dynamics across multiple neural cell types including neurons, astrocytes, oligodendrocytes and microglia.
Core methods
The study characterized viral replication dynamics, transmission mechanisms, viral evolution, and host immune responses in brain organoids, focusing on viral spread through budding and cell-to-cell transmission, and analysis of defective viral genomes and inflammatory mediator production.
Main results
Ebola virus infected multiple neural cell types in human brain organoids, establishing persistent infection associated with viral evolution, accumulation of defective viral genomes, and production of pro-inflammatory mediators and type I interferons by activated microglia.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted that immune privilege enables Ebola virus persistence by limiting pathogen clearance while allowing chronic low-level replication, with the novel finding that this persistence leads to chronic neuroinflammation and neuronal injury through viral evolution and immune activation.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings inform surveillance strategies for Ebola virus persistence in the central nervous system, highlight the need for therapies targeting viral reservoirs in immune-privileged sites, and suggest potential neurological complications in survivors.
Limitations and evidence strength
The evidence strength is moderate due to the use of organoid models which may not fully replicate in vivo complexity, though the study provides direct mechanistic insights into Ebola virus persistence in neural tissue.

反应性干预措施后感染预防和控制实践改进的持久性有限,导致医疗机构容易受到 埃博拉病毒 传播 的影响。

Limited Durability of Improvements in Infection Prevention and Control Practices Following Reactive Interventions Leaves Healthcare Facilities Vulnerable to Ebola Virus Transmission.
作者： Joy Yang, Kasereka Masumbuko Claude, Emily Kimani, Michael T Hawkes
摘要中文翻译我们评估了基伍/伊图里 埃博拉病毒 暴发(2018-2020 年)期间感染预防和控制 (IPC) 捆绑干预的影响和持久性。干预后 6 个月,IPC 评分先上升,然后下降(中位数 19/36、30/36 和 28/36,P < .0001)。如果没有持续的 IPC,医疗机构在未来仍然容易受到院内 传播 的影响。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在评估基伍/伊图里埃博拉病毒 暴发(2018-2020)期间感染预防和控制(IPC)实践的有效性和可持续性。这项研究的动机是了解如何维持 IPC 改进以防止 传播 或 埃博拉病毒 的院内感染。
设计与对象
该研究是在基伍/伊图里 埃博拉病毒 暴发(2018-2020 年)期间进行的,评估了 IPC 捆绑干预对卫生机构的影响。干预后不同时间点的中位 IPC 评分分别为 19/36、30/36 和 28/36。
核心方法
该研究采用前后设计来评估 IPC 评分,在基线、干预后 6 个月和干预后 12 个月进行评估。IPC 分数使用标准化评分系统进行测量。
主要结果
IPC 评分在干预后有所增加,但在干预后 6 个月有所下降(中位数 19/36、30/36 和 28/36,P < .0001)。
解释与创新
研究结果表明,如果没有持续的 IPC 努力,卫生机构仍然容易受到未来 埃博拉病毒 暴发 的影响。这凸显了持续监测和加强 IPC 实践的必要性。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果对于医疗机构制定长期 IPC 策略具有重要意义,以降低 埃博拉病毒 暴发 期间院内 传播 的风险。
局限与证据强度
该研究因其对单一时间点评估的依赖以及自我报告的 IPC 分数可能存在偏差而受到限制。由于这些限制,证据的强度中等。

Limited Durability of Improvements in Infection Prevention and Control Practices Following Reactive Interventions Leaves Healthcare Facilities Vulnerable to Ebola Virus Transmission.

Authors: Joy Yang, Kasereka Masumbuko Claude, Emily Kimani, Michael T Hawkes
Original abstract (English)
We assessed the impact and durability of an infection prevention and control (IPC) bundle intervention during the Kivu/Ituri Ebola virus outbreak (2018-2020). IPC scores increased, then declined 6 months postintervention (median 19/36, 30/36, and 28/36, P < .0001). Without sustained IPC, health facilities remain vulnerable to nosocomial transmission in future outbreaks.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to evaluate the effectiveness and sustainability of infection prevention and control (IPC) practices during the Kivu/Ituri Ebola virus outbreak (2018-2020). The research was motivated by the need to understand how IPC improvements could be maintained to prevent nosocomial transmission of Ebola viruses.
Design and population
The study was conducted during the Kivu/Ituri Ebola virus outbreak (2018-2020) and assessed the impact of an IPC bundle intervention on health facilities. The median IPC scores were 19/36, 30/36, and 28/36 at different time points postintervention.
Core methods
The study used a before-and-after design to evaluate IPC scores, with assessments conducted at baseline, 6 months postintervention, and 12 months postintervention. IPC scores were measured using a standardized scoring system.
Main results
IPC scores increased after the intervention but declined 6 months postintervention (median 19/36, 30/36, and 28/36, P < .0001).
Interpretation and novelty
The findings suggest that without sustained IPC efforts, health facilities remain vulnerable to future Ebola virus outbreaks. This highlights the need for ongoing monitoring and reinforcement of IPC practices.
Scientific and public-health significance
The results have important implications for the development of long-term IPC strategies in healthcare facilities to mitigate the risk of nosocomial transmission during Ebola virus outbreaks.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its reliance on a single time point assessment and potential for bias in self-reported IPC scores. The evidence is moderate in strength due to these limitations.

2007 年、2012 年和 2026 年本迪布焦埃博拉病毒糖蛋白的时空比较 进化 暴发

Comparative Time–Space Evolution of Bundibugyo Ebolavirus Glycoprotein Across the 2007, 2012, and 2026 Outbreaks
作者： Young-Chul Park
摘要中文翻译摘要 本迪布焦埃博拉病毒(BDBV)是公认的人类致病性埃博拉病毒之一,已在乌干达(2007年)、刚果民主共和国(2012年)和刚果民主共和国/乌干达(2026年)引起三起暴发病例。尽管可以从这些 暴发 中获得完整的糖蛋白 (GP) 序列,但整合其时间、地理和功能进化特征的综合比较分析仍然有限。本研究利用统一的氨基酸坐标框架对代表三个暴发时期的54条完整GP氨基酸序列(676个氨基酸)进行了比较分析。完整的序列比对可以直接比较所有分离株的同源残基,无需重新编号坐标。核心氨基酸取代位点被确定,根据其进化行为进行分类,并映射到已建立的 GP 功能域上。比较分析确定了分布在整个 GP 序列中的 21 个核心氨基酸取代位点。这些替换被分为五种进化模式:固定(n = 10)、回复(n = 4)、出现(n = 2)、暴发 内多态性(n = 4)和罕见变异(n = 1)。大多数取代集中在粘蛋白样结构域 (MLD) 和 GP1 C 末端区域内,而弗林蛋白酶切割位点、内部融合环、免疫抑制基序和跨膜结构域在所有三个 暴发 时期保持完全保守。时间比较表明,GP 进化 主要通过氨基酸取代而不是插入或删除发生,表明尽管病毒 进化 持续存在,但仍存在大量结构保守。这些发现提供了 BDBV GP 进化 跨三个记录的 暴发 的综合时空概述,并建立了监测未来遗传变异的标准化比较框架。这里提出的分析方法可能有助于 基因组监测 并支持未来对埃博拉病毒 进化、抗原多样性和潜在治疗靶点的比较研究。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本迪布焦埃博拉病毒 (BDBV) 是公认的人类致病性埃博拉病毒之一,已在乌干达(2007 年)、刚果民主共和国(2012 年)和刚果民主共和国/乌干达(2026 年)引起了三起记录在案的 暴发。
设计与对象
尽管可以从这些 暴发 中获得完整的糖蛋白 (GP) 序列,但整合其时间、地理和功能进化特征的综合比较分析仍然有限。
核心方法
本研究利用统一的氨基酸坐标框架对代表三个暴发时期的54条完整GP氨基酸序列(676个氨基酸)进行了比较分析。
主要结果
比较分析确定了分布在整个 GP 序列中的 21 个核心氨基酸取代位点。
解释与创新
这些发现提供了 BDBV GP 进化 跨三个记录的 暴发 的综合时空概述,并建立了监测未来遗传变异的标准化比较框架。
科研与公卫意义
这里提出的分析方法可能有助于 基因组监测 并支持未来对埃博拉病毒 进化、抗原多样性和潜在治疗靶点的比较研究。
局限与证据强度
时间比较表明,GP 进化 主要通过氨基酸取代而不是插入或删除发生,表明尽管病毒 进化 持续存在,但仍存在大量结构保守。

Comparative Time–Space Evolution of Bundibugyo Ebolavirus Glycoprotein Across the 2007, 2012, and 2026 Outbreaks

Authors: Young-Chul Park
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Bundibugyo ebolavirus (BDBV) is one of the recognized human-pathogenic ebolaviruses and has caused three documented outbreaks in Uganda (2007), the Democratic Republic of the Congo (2012), and the Democratic Republic of the Congo/Uganda (2026). Although complete glycoprotein (GP) sequences are available from these outbreaks, comprehensive comparative analyses integrating their temporal, geographical, and functional evolutionary characteristics remain limited. In this study, 54 complete GP amino acid sequences (676 amino acids) representing the three outbreak periods were comparatively analyzed using a unified amino acid coordinate framework. Complete sequence alignment enabled direct comparison of homologous residues across all isolates without coordinate renumbering. Core amino acid substitution sites were identified, classified according to their evolutionary behavior, and mapped onto established GP functional domains. Comparative analysis identified 21 core amino acid substitution sites distributed throughout the GP sequence. These substitutions were classified into five evolutionary patterns: fixation (n = 10), reversion (n = 4), emergence (n = 2), within-outbreak polymorphism (n = 4), and a rare variant (n = 1). Most substitutions were concentrated within the mucin-like domain (MLD) and the GP1 C-terminal region, whereas the furin cleavage site, internal fusion loop, immunosuppressive motif, and transmembrane domain remained completely conserved throughout all three outbreak periods. Temporal comparison demonstrated that GP evolution occurred primarily through amino acid substitutions rather than insertions or deletions, indicating substantial structural conservation despite continued viral evolution. These findings provide an integrated time–space overview of BDBV GP evolution across the three documented outbreaks and establish a standardized comparative framework for monitoring future genetic variation. The analytical approach presented here may facilitate genomic surveillance and support future comparative investigations of ebolavirus evolution, antigenic diversity, and potential therapeutic targets.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Bundibugyo ebolavirus (BDBV) is one of the recognized human-pathogenic ebolaviruses and has caused three documented outbreaks in Uganda (2007), the Democratic Republic of the Congo (2012), and the Democratic Republic of the Congo/Uganda (2026).
Design and population
Although complete glycoprotein (GP) sequences are available from these outbreaks, comprehensive comparative analyses integrating their temporal, geographical, and functional evolutionary characteristics remain limited.
Core methods
In this study, 54 complete GP amino acid sequences (676 amino acids) representing the three outbreak periods were comparatively analyzed using a unified amino acid coordinate framework.
Main results
Comparative analysis identified 21 core amino acid substitution sites distributed throughout the GP sequence.
Interpretation and novelty
These findings provide an integrated time–space overview of BDBV GP evolution across the three documented outbreaks and establish a standardized comparative framework for monitoring future genetic variation.
Scientific and public-health significance
The analytical approach presented here may facilitate genomic surveillance and support future comparative investigations of ebolavirus evolution, antigenic diversity, and potential therapeutic targets.
Limitations and evidence strength
Temporal comparison demonstrated that GP evolution occurred primarily through amino acid substitutions rather than insertions or deletions, indicating substantial structural conservation despite continued viral evolution.

埃博拉病毒 利用 宿主 lncRNA LINC01740 增强 ATF3 并抑制 抗病毒药物 免疫应答 2259953

Ebola virus exploits host lncRNA LINC01740 to enhance ATF3 and suppress antiviral immune responses 2259953
作者： Narasimha Tanuj Gunturu, Anu Gopal, Marija Djurkovic-Lopez, Daniela Serrano, Asha Thomas, Urmi Thaker, Olena Shtanko, Smita Kulkarni
摘要中文翻译摘要 简介 埃博拉病毒 (EBOV) 会导致高度致命的出血热,其特征是细胞因子释放失控和 抗病毒药物 防御受损。埃博拉病毒主要感染人类巨噬细胞,其中不受控制的复制会重新编程 宿主 转录并加剧过度炎症。长链非编码 RNA (lncRNA) 是转录和免疫的有效调节因子,在 EBOV 发病机制 中仍未得到充分探索。我们在原代人巨噬细胞中鉴定了 EBOV 诱导的 lncRNA 与转录因子 (TF) 共同调节,揭示了 EBOV 重新连接 宿主 基因表达以促进免疫抑制和持久性的新机制。方法使用高毒力 EBOV(Mayinga 变体),感染了来自 9 个供体的原代人巨噬细胞。我们比较了感染 24 小时和 48 小时后 EBOV 感染与 MOCK 感染巨噬细胞的蛋白质编码和 lncRNA 转录组。差异表达的lncRNA与邻近的蛋白质编码基因相关,以推断顺式调控关系。使用反义寡核苷酸、CRISPR 干扰 (CRISPRi) 和诱导型 CRISPR-Cas13d 敲低进行功能验证,然后进行 qPCR、RNA-seq 和蛋白质印迹结果 EBOV 感染引发巨噬细胞转录组的广泛重塑。LINC01740 被强烈诱导并与其邻近基因 ATF3 紧密共表达,ATF3 是一种已知可抑制 抗病毒药物 信号传导的应激反应转录因子。LINC01740 敲除降低了巨噬细胞中 ATF3 的表达。ATF3 抑制恢复了干扰素 β 的产生,增强了先天免疫激活,并在 EBOV 感染的巨噬细胞中诱导了强烈的 抗病毒药物 反应。我们的数据显示 ATF3 损害巨噬细胞中的 IFN-I 反应,导致免疫麻痹 结论我们的数据显示 LINC01740 是一种新型 宿主 lncRNA,与 EBOV 共同选择增强 ATF3 并抑制 抗病毒药物 反应。在这里,我们展示了一种新型 LINC01740—ATF3—IFN-I 调节轴,可驱动免疫逃避和病毒持久性,强调 lncRNA 作为潜在的 抗病毒药物 治疗靶点 资金来源 不适用 主题类别 病毒免疫学 (VIR)
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
埃博拉病毒 (EBOV) 会导致高度致命的出血热,其特征是细胞因子释放失控和 抗病毒药物 防御受损,而 EBOV 发病机制 中的长非编码 RNA (lncRNA) 仍未被探索。
设计与对象
研究人员使用高毒力的 EBOV(Mayinga 变种)感染了来自 9 名捐赠者的原代人类巨噬细胞,并在感染后 24 小时和 48 小时比较了 EBOV 感染与 MOCK 感染的巨噬细胞的转录组。
核心方法
将差异表达的 lncRNA 与相邻的蛋白质编码基因相关联,以推断顺式调控关系,并使用反义寡核苷酸、CRISPR 干扰 (CRISPRi) 和诱导型 CRISPR-Cas13d 敲低进行功能验证,然后进行 qPCR、RNA-seq 和 Western blotting。
主要结果
埃博拉病毒感染引发巨噬细胞转录组的广泛重塑;LINC01740 被强烈诱导并与 ATF3 紧密共表达,LINC01740 敲除降低了 ATF3 表达,而 ATF3 抑制则恢复了干扰素 β 的产生并增强了 抗病毒药物 反应。
解释与创新
该研究揭示了一种新的 LINC01740-ATF3-IFN-I 调节轴,可驱动免疫逃避和病毒持续存在,强调 lncRNA 作为潜在的 抗病毒药物 治疗靶点。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,靶向LINC01740-ATF3轴可以恢复抗病毒药物感染中的抗病毒药物免疫力,为治疗干预提供新策略。
局限与证据强度
由于使用了原代人类巨噬细胞和多种验证方法,证据强度很高,尽管该研究仅限于体外实验,可能无法完全反映体内的复杂性。

Ebola virus exploits host lncRNA LINC01740 to enhance ATF3 and suppress antiviral immune responses 2259953

Authors: Narasimha Tanuj Gunturu, Anu Gopal, Marija Djurkovic-Lopez, Daniela Serrano, Asha Thomas, Urmi Thaker, Olena Shtanko, Smita Kulkarni
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Introduction Ebola virus (EBOV) causes a highly lethal hemorrhagic fever marked by uncontrolled cytokine release and impaired antiviral defense. EBOV primarily infects human macrophages, where uncontrolled replication reprograms host transcription and fuels hyperinflammation. Long non-coding RNAs (lncRNAs), potent regulators of transcription and immunity, remain largely unexplored in EBOV pathogenesis. We identified EBOV-induced lncRNAs co-regulated with transcription factors (TFs) in primary human macrophages, revealing a novel mechanism by which EBOV rewires host gene expression to promote immune suppression and persistence Methods Using the highly virulent EBOV (Mayinga variant), we infected primary human macrophages from nine donors. We compared protein-coding and lncRNA transcriptomes of EBOV-infected vs. MOCK-infected macrophages at 24- and 48-hours post-infection. Differentially expressed lncRNAs were correlated with adjacent protein-coding genes to infer cis-regulatory relationships. Functional validation used antisense oligonucleotides, CRISPR interference (CRISPRi), and inducible CRISPR—Cas13d knockdown, followed by qPCR, RNA-seq, and Western blotting Results EBOV infection triggered extensive remodeling of macrophage transcriptome. LINC01740 was strongly induced and tightly co-expressed with its neighboring gene, ATF3, a stress-responsive transcription factor known to repress antiviral signaling. LINC01740 knockdown reduced ATF3 expression in macrophages. ATF3 inhibition restored interferon-beta production, enhanced innate immune activation, and induced strong antiviral responses in EBOV-infected macrophages. Our data show that ATF3 impairs IFN-I responses in macrophages, contributing to immune paralysis Conclusion Our data revealed LINC01740 as a novel host lncRNA co-opted by EBOV to enhance ATF3 and suppress antiviral responses. Here we show a novel LINC01740—ATF3—IFN-I regulatory axis driving immune evasion and viral persistence, highlighting lncRNAs as potential antiviral therapeutic targets Funding Source n/a Topic Categories Viral Immunology (VIR)
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Ebola virus (EBOV) causes a highly lethal hemorrhagic fever marked by uncontrolled cytokine release and impaired antiviral defense, and long non-coding RNAs (lncRNAs) remain largely unexplored in EBOV pathogenesis.
Design and population
Using the highly virulent EBOV (Mayinga variant), the researchers infected primary human macrophages from nine donors and compared transcriptomes of EBOV-infected vs. MOCK-infected macrophages at 24- and 48-hours post-infection.
Core methods
Differentially expressed lncRNAs were correlated with adjacent protein-coding genes to infer cis-regulatory relationships, and functional validation used antisense oligonucleotides, CRISPR interference (CRISPRi), and inducible CRISPR—Cas13d knockdown, followed by qPCR, RNA-seq, and Western blotting.
Main results
EBOV infection triggered extensive remodeling of the macrophage transcriptome; LINC01740 was strongly induced and tightly co-expressed with ATF3, and LINC01740 knockdown reduced ATF3 expression while ATF3 inhibition restored interferon-beta production and enhanced antiviral responses.
Interpretation and novelty
The study revealed a novel LINC01740—ATF3—IFN-I regulatory axis driving immune evasion and viral persistence, highlighting lncRNAs as potential antiviral therapeutic targets.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest that targeting the LINC01740—ATF3 axis could restore antiviral immunity in Ebola virus infection, offering a new strategy for therapeutic intervention.
Limitations and evidence strength
The evidence strength is high due to the use of primary human macrophages and multiple validation methods, though the study is limited to in vitro experiments and may not fully reflect in vivo complexity.

本迪布焦埃博拉病毒 暴发 响应的环和社区 疫苗接种:随机网络建模研究

Ring and community vaccination for Bundibugyo ebolavirus outbreak response: a stochastic network modelling study
作者： Andrews, J. R., Placide, M., MUKADI, P., Kindrachuk, J., Hoff, N. A., Rimoin, A. W., Bogoch, I.
摘要中文翻译背景:疫苗接种 与 rVSV-ZEBOV 对扎伊尔埃博拉病毒非常有效,但对本迪布焦埃博拉病毒 (BDBV) 的保护作用尚未得到证实。我们评估了 BDBV 暴发 期间在实际操作限制下部分交叉保护假设 疫苗 的相对人群影响和剂量效率。方法:我们在两层家庭-社区联系网络上开发了一个随机 传播 模型,该模型根据 2026 年刚果民主共和国 BDBV 暴发 数据进行校准。使用贝叶斯更新模型估计随时间变化的有效繁殖数。我们评估了病例检测、隔离、接触者追踪、反应性 环形疫苗接种 和社区 疫苗接种(20-80% 覆盖率)。基本情况 疫苗 功效为 45%,包括针对疾病和 死亡率 的暴露后保护。主要结果是 90 天内 死亡率 和 发病率 的减少、总剂量和剂量效率(避免死亡的剂量),通过概率敏感性分析对每个策略进行 10,000 次匹配的随机重复进行评估。结果:与单独的基本操作(30% 检测、30% 跟踪)相比,单独的增强操作(70% 检测、80% 跟踪)将预期 死亡率 减少了 81.9% (95% UI 72.3-88.8)。基础操作下的反应环 2 疫苗接种 将 死亡率 减少了 7.8% (4.7-10.9),每次避免死亡需要 89.5 剂。添加增强型操作后,Ring 2 疫苗接种 总体将 死亡率 减少了 82.5% (73.0-88.9),比单独增强型操作增加了 2.7% (0.9-4.9) 的增量效益。社区 疫苗接种 在 20%、40%、60% 和 80% 覆盖率下,死亡率 降低了 44.4% (33.9-51.8)、67.9% (56.7-75.6)、80.4% (70.8-85.9) 和 87.2% (79.6-91.3),需要每次避免死亡需要 40.3 至 80.9 剂剂量。解读:即使没有 疫苗接种,加强病例发现、接触者追踪和隔离也能避免大多数死亡。一旦这些操作变得强大,反应性 环形疫苗接种 就几乎没有增加任何进一步的好处,而快速社区 疫苗接种 在模拟场景中产生了最大的减少,但需要更多的剂量。部分保护性 BDBV 疫苗 的群体水平价值将主要取决于快速、广泛的交付。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
疫苗接种 与 rVSV-ZEBOV 对扎伊尔埃博拉病毒非常有效,但对本迪布焦埃博拉病毒 (BDBV) 的保护作用尚未得到证实。该研究评估了 BDBV 暴发 期间部分交叉保护假设 疫苗 的相对人群影响和剂量效率。
设计与对象
我们在两层家庭-社区联系网络上开发了随机 传播 模型,并根据 2026 年刚果民主共和国 BDBV 暴发 数据进行校准。基本情况 疫苗 功效为 45%,包括针对疾病和 死亡率 的暴露后保护。
核心方法

主要结果
与单独的基本操作(30% 检测、30% 跟踪)相比,单独的增强操作(70% 检测、80% 跟踪)将预期 死亡率 减少了 81.9% (95% UI 72.3-88.8)。基础操作下的反应环 2 疫苗接种 将 死亡率 减少了 7.8% (4.7-10.9),每次避免死亡需要 89.5 剂。社区 疫苗接种 在 20%、40%、60% 和 80% 覆盖率下,死亡率 降低了 44.4% (33.9-51.8)、67.9% (56.7-75.6)、80.4% (70.8-85.9) 和 87.2% (79.6-91.3),需要每次避免死亡需要 40.3 至 80.9 剂剂量。

科研与公卫意义
部分保护性 BDBV 疫苗 的群体水平价值将主要取决于快速、广泛的交付。研究结果通过强调环形和社区 疫苗接种 方法之间的权衡,为 暴发 响应策略提供信息。
局限与证据强度
该研究依赖于具有 45% 功效的假设 疫苗 和建模场景,而不是现实世界的实施。由于依赖模拟数据以及有关 疫苗 功效和操作限制的假设,证据强度中等。

Ring and community vaccination for Bundibugyo ebolavirus outbreak response: a stochastic network modelling study

Authors: Andrews, J. R., Placide, M., MUKADI, P., Kindrachuk, J., Hoff, N. A., Rimoin, A. W., Bogoch, I.
Original abstract (English)
Background: Vaccination with rVSV-ZEBOV is highly effective against Zaire ebolavirus, but protection against Bundibugyo ebolavirus (BDBV) is unproven. We evaluated the relative population impact and dose efficiency of a partially cross-protective hypothetical vaccine under operationally realistic constraints during a BDBV outbreak. Methods: We developed a stochastic transmission model on a two-layer household-community contact network calibrated to 2026 Democratic Republic of the Congo BDBV outbreak data. Time-varying effective reproduction numbers were estimated using a Bayesian renewal model. We evaluated case detection, isolation, contact tracing, reactive ring vaccination, and community vaccination (20-80% coverage). Base-case vaccine efficacy was 45% and included post-exposure protection against disease and mortality. Primary outcomes were mortality and incidence reductions, total doses, and dose efficiency (doses per death averted) over 90 days, evaluated in a probabilistic sensitivity analysis with 10,000 matched stochastic replicates per strategy. Results: Compared with base operations alone (30% detection, 30% tracing), enhanced operations alone (70% detection, 80% tracing) reduced expected mortality by 81.9% (95% UI 72.3-88.8). Reactive Ring 2 vaccination under base operations reduced mortality by 7.8% (4.7-10.9), requiring 89.5 doses per death averted. Added to enhanced operations, Ring 2 vaccination reduced mortality by 82.5% overall (73.0-88.9), an incremental benefit of 2.7% (0.9-4.9) beyond enhanced operations alone. Community vaccination at 20%, 40%, 60%, and 80% coverage reduced mortality by 44.4% (33.9-51.8), 67.9% (56.7-75.6), 80.4% (70.8-85.9), and 87.2% (79.6-91.3), requiring 40.3 to 80.9 doses per death averted. Interpretation: Strengthened case finding, contact tracing, and isolation averted most deaths even without vaccination. Once these operations were strong, reactive ring vaccination added little further benefit, whereas rapid community vaccination produced the largest reductions in simulated scenarios but required substantially more doses. A partially protective BDBV vaccine's population-level value will depend principally on rapid, broad delivery.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Vaccination with rVSV-ZEBOV is highly effective against Zaire ebolavirus, but protection against Bundibugyo ebolavirus (BDBV) is unproven. The study evaluated the relative population impact and dose efficiency of a partially cross-protective hypothetical vaccine during a BDBV outbreak.
Design and population
We developed a stochastic transmission model on a two-layer household-community contact network calibrated to 2026 Democratic Republic of the Congo BDBV outbreak data. Base-case vaccine efficacy was 45% and included post-exposure protection against disease and mortality.
Core methods
Time-varying effective reproduction numbers were estimated using a Bayesian renewal model. Primary outcomes were mortality and incidence reductions, total doses, and dose efficiency (doses per death averted) over 90 days, evaluated in a probabilistic sensitivity analysis with 10,000 matched stochastic replicates per strategy.
Main results
Compared with base operations alone (30% detection, 30% tracing), enhanced operations alone (70% detection, 80% tracing) reduced expected mortality by 81.9% (95% UI 72.3-88.8). Reactive Ring 2 vaccination under base operations reduced mortality by 7.8% (4.7-10.9), requiring 89.5 doses per death averted. Community vaccination at 20%, 40%, 60%, and 80% coverage reduced mortality by 44.4% (33.9-51.8), 67.9% (56.7-75.6), 80.4% (70.8-85.9), and 87.2% (79.6-91.3), requiring 40.3 to 80.9 doses per death averted.
Interpretation and novelty
Strengthened case finding, contact tracing, and isolation averted most deaths even without vaccination. Once these operations were strong, reactive ring vaccination added little further benefit, whereas rapid community vaccination produced the largest reductions in simulated scenarios but required substantially more doses.
Scientific and public-health significance
A partially protective BDBV vaccine's population-level value will depend principally on rapid, broad delivery. The findings inform outbreak response strategies by highlighting the trade-offs between ring and community vaccination approaches.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study relied on a hypothetical vaccine with 45% efficacy and modeled scenarios rather than real-world implementation. The evidence strength is moderate due to the reliance on simulated data and assumptions about vaccine efficacy and operational constraints.

B 常规重要学术文献 · 研究B routine priorityAcademic literature · Research

针对由扎伊伦斯正博拉病毒引起的 暴发 或 埃博拉病毒病 的地理定向活动与 环形疫苗接种 活动的成本效益

Cost-effectiveness of geographically targeted versus ring vaccination campaigns in response to an outbreak of Ebola Virus Disease caused by Orthoebolavirus zairense
作者： Nedjati-Gilani, G., Cox, V. M., Pearson, C. A. B., Skarp, J. E., Johnson, R., Ebengo, D. M., Mbala-Kingebeni, P., Niyukuri, D., Morgenstern, C., Hinsley, W.
摘要中文翻译背景世界卫生组织建议 埃博拉病毒病 (EVD) 病例接触链的 环形疫苗接种 (RV) 包含扎伊尔塞正埃博拉病毒 暴发。然而,在偏远地区或受冲突影响的环境中,有效的接触者追踪具有挑战性,这一策略可能会动摇。有针对性的地理 疫苗接种 (TGV)——对埃博拉病毒病病例地点附近的每个人进行免疫接种,无需追踪接触者——已作为替代方案实施。我们比较了不同 暴发 情景下 TGV 与 RV 的流行病学影响和成本效益。方法我们扩展了一个基于空间显式个体的随机 EVD 传播 模型,该模型在刚果民主共和国校准为 暴发。我们模拟了改变流行病学和操作参数的 暴发。我们评估了 疫苗接种 策略的总病例数、死亡人数、流行持续时间、使用的 疫苗 剂量和增量净货币效益 (INMB)。结果没有一种策略能够在所有情况下占主导地位。为了获得更好的总体反应表现,RV 导致的病例和死亡人数比 TGV 少得多(大约减少 20% 的病例),需要的剂量更少,并且 INMB 更高。在悲观反应假设下,TGV 通过预防更多感染、抑制最大 暴发 和使用更少剂量而略微优于 RV,尽管策略之间的 INMB 相似。解读不同的运营环境推荐不同的疫苗接种策略。总体而言,监测、接触者追踪和隔离对扎伊尔塞正博拉病毒 暴发 控制影响最大。根据响应有效性,添加 疫苗接种 的首选策略会有所不同。在有效的病例检测和管理的支持下,RV 似乎是更好的方法。在严重的响应限制下,TGV 可以更加有效。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
这项研究需要解决在接触者追踪困难的环境中包含 埃博拉病毒病 暴发 的挑战,比较 环形疫苗接种 和有针对性的地理 疫苗接种 策略的有效性。
设计与对象
我们扩展了一个基于空间显式个体的随机 EVD 传播 模型,该模型在刚果民主共和国校准为 暴发,模拟 暴发 变化的流行病学和操作参数。
核心方法
我们使用空间显式的基于个体的随机 EVD 传播 模型评估了 疫苗接种 策略的总病例数、死亡人数、流行病持续时间、使用的 疫苗 剂量和增量净货币效益 (INMB)。
主要结果
没有一种策略能够在所有场景中占主导地位;RV 导致的病例和死亡人数比 TGV 少得多(大约减少 20% 的病例),需要的剂量更少,并且 INMB 更频繁地更高,而在悲观反应假设下,TGV 通过预防更多感染和使用更少的剂量,略优于 RV。
解释与创新
不同的运营环境推荐不同的疫苗接种策略;根据响应有效性,添加 疫苗接种 的首选策略会有所不同,RV 在有效的病例检测和管理的支持下显得更好,而 TGV 在严格的响应限制下更有效。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,暴发、接触者追踪和隔离对扎伊尔正埃博拉病毒 暴发 控制影响最大,并且 暴发 策略选择应根据操作环境和响应有效性进行定制,从而为 暴发 响应策略提供信息。
局限与证据强度
证据强度中等,因为该研究依赖于针对特定 暴发 校准的模型,这可能会限制对所有 暴发 设置的普遍性,尽管该模型的空间明确设计和对不同场景的考虑增强了其与决策的相关性。

Cost-effectiveness of geographically targeted versus ring vaccination campaigns in response to an outbreak of Ebola Virus Disease caused by Orthoebolavirus zairense

Authors: Nedjati-Gilani, G., Cox, V. M., Pearson, C. A. B., Skarp, J. E., Johnson, R., Ebengo, D. M., Mbala-Kingebeni, P., Niyukuri, D., Morgenstern, C., Hinsley, W.
Original abstract (English)
BackgroundWHO recommends ring vaccination (RV) of Ebola virus disease (EVD) case contact chains to contain outbreaks of Orthoebolavirus zairense. However, this strategy can falter in remote or conflict-affected settings where effective contact tracing is challenging. Targeted geographic vaccination (TGV) - immunising everyone near an EVD cases location without requiring contact tracing - has been implemented as an alternative. We compare the epidemiological impact and cost-effectiveness of TGV versus RV under diverse outbreak scenarios. MethodsWe extended a spatially-explicit individual-based stochastic EVD transmission model calibrated to outbreaks in the Democratic Republic of the Congo. We simulated outbreaks varying both epidemiological and operational parameters. We evaluated vaccination strategies on total cases, deaths, epidemic duration, vaccine doses used, and incremental net monetary benefit (INMB). ResultsNo single strategy dominates in all scenarios. For better overall response performance, RV results in substantially fewer cases and deaths than TGV (approximately 20% fewer cases), requires fewer doses, and has higher INMB more frequently. Under pessimistic response assumptions, TGV slightly outperforms RV by preventing more infections, curbing the largest outbreaks, and using fewer doses, although the INMBs are similar between strategies. InterpretationDifferent operational contexts recommend different vaccination strategies. Overall, surveillance, contact tracing, and isolation have the most impact on Orthoebolavirus zairense outbreak control. Depending on response effectiveness, the preferred strategy for adding vaccination varies. Backed by effective case detection and management, RV appears the better approach. Under severe response constraints, TGV can be more effective.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study was needed to address the challenge of containing Ebola virus disease outbreaks in settings where contact tracing is difficult, comparing the effectiveness of ring vaccination and targeted geographic vaccination strategies.
Design and population
We extended a spatially-explicit individual-based stochastic EVD transmission model calibrated to outbreaks in the Democratic Republic of the Congo, simulating outbreaks varying both epidemiological and operational parameters.
Core methods
We evaluated vaccination strategies on total cases, deaths, epidemic duration, vaccine doses used, and incremental net monetary benefit (INMB) using a spatially-explicit individual-based stochastic EVD transmission model.
Main results
No single strategy dominates in all scenarios; RV results in substantially fewer cases and deaths than TGV (approximately 20% fewer cases), requires fewer doses, and has higher INMB more frequently, while under pessimistic response assumptions, TGV slightly outperforms RV by preventing more infections and using fewer doses.
Interpretation and novelty
Different operational contexts recommend different vaccination strategies; depending on response effectiveness, the preferred strategy for adding vaccination varies, with RV appearing better when backed by effective case detection and management, and TGV being more effective under severe response constraints.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings inform outbreak response strategies by demonstrating that surveillance, contact tracing, and isolation have the most impact on Orthoebolavirus zairense outbreak control, and that vaccination strategy choice should be tailored to operational context and response effectiveness.
Limitations and evidence strength
The evidence strength is moderate as the study relies on a model calibrated to specific outbreaks, which may limit generalizability to all outbreak settings, though the model's spatially-explicit design and consideration of diverse scenarios enhance its relevance for decision-making.

评估机场针对本迪布焦埃博拉病毒病进行症状筛查的有效性

Estimating the effectiveness of syndromic screening at airports for Bundibugyo ebolavirus disease
作者： Quilty, B. J., CMMID Bundibugyo Working Group,
摘要中文翻译我们使用随机模拟模型来估计机场出入境和/或入境本迪布焦埃博拉病毒病 (BVD) 综合征筛查的有效性。对于从刚果民主共和国或乌干达起飞的 12 小时国际航班,筛查灵敏度为 86%,大约 73% 的感染者会逃避出境和入境联合筛查(95% CrI:68-77%)。在成功登机的人中,大约 92% 的人会在不被发现的情况下抵达(95% CrI:90-94%)。这一较高的未被发现比例反映了潜伏期的相对持续时间(约 7.7 天;Wamala 等人,20101)和发病到严重疾病的间隔时间(约 4 天;Funk & Abbott 20262):大多数受感染的旅客在症状出现前登机,并且任何症状筛查都无法检测到,而那些有症状的旅客则迅速发展为严重到无法旅行的旅客。由于不发烧的确诊病例比例本身是不确定的,我们还报告了一种保守的情况,即所有确诊病例都发热(0%无症状当量),在此情况下,未检测到的比例仍然很高,约为 64%(95% CrI:57-70%)。在流行病活跃增长期间,这种情况变得更加复杂,因为最近暴露的(因此出现症状前的)病例在旅行者中所占比例过高。症状性机场筛查对通过航空旅行传播 BVD 提供的保护有限,应辅之以 暴发 源头控制、在与受影响地区旅行联系密切的接收国加强临床 监测,以及为来自受影响地区的旅行者提供出发后自我监测指导。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在评估机场对本迪布焦埃博拉病毒病(BVD)进行症状筛查的有效性,以应对防止其通过国际航空旅行传播的公共卫生挑战。
设计与对象
该研究使用随机模拟模型来评估从刚果民主共和国或乌干达起飞的 12 小时国际航班上的 BVD 出境和/或入境综合征筛查。
核心方法
该研究采用随机模拟模型来估计筛查有效性,结合潜伏期(约7.7天)和发病到重症间隔(约4天)等参数,并分析了不同比例发热病例的情况。
主要结果
在筛查敏感度为 86% 的情况下,大约 73% 的受感染旅客将逃避出境和入境联合筛查(95% CrI:68-77%),而在登机的旅客中,大约 92% 的人抵达时不会被发现(95% CrI:90-94%)。即使在所有确诊病例均发热的保守情况下,未检测到的比例仍然很高,约为 64%(95% CrI:57-70%)。
解释与创新
作者将未发现的高比例解释为反映了潜伏期和发病到严重疾病间隔的相对持续时间,并指出大多数受感染的旅行者在症状出现之前登机,而有症状的个体进展太快而无法旅行。新颖之处在于量化现实流行条件下 BVD 综合征筛查的局限性。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,综合症机场筛查对通过航空旅行传播 BVD 的保护有限,应辅之以 暴发 源头控制、在高连通性接收国加强临床 监测 以及对来自受影响地区的旅行者的出发后自我监测指导。
局限与证据强度
证据强度中等,因为该研究依赖于对潜伏期和症状表现进行假设的模拟模型,并且不发烧的确诊病例的比例仍然不确定,这可能会影响结果的普遍性。

Estimating the effectiveness of syndromic screening at airports for Bundibugyo ebolavirus disease

Authors: Quilty, B. J., CMMID Bundibugyo Working Group,
Original abstract (English)
We used a stochastic simulation model to estimate the effectiveness of exit and/or entry syndromic screening at airports for Bundibugyo ebolavirus disease (BVD). For a 12-hour international flight from DRC or Uganda at 86% screening sensitivity, approximately 73% of infected people presenting for travel would evade combined exit and entry screening (95% CrI: 68-77%). Among those who successfully boarded, approximately 92% would arrive without detection (95% CrI: 90-94%). This high undetected fraction reflects the relative durations of the incubation period (approximately 7.7 days; Wamala et al. 20101) and the onset-to-severe-disease interval (approximately 4 days; Funk & Abbott 20262): most infected travellers board before symptom onset and are undetectable by any syndromic screen, whilst those who are symptomatic progress rapidly to illness severe enough to preclude travel. Because the proportion of confirmed cases presenting without fever is itself uncertain, we also report a conservative scenario in which all confirmed cases are febrile (0% asymptomatic-equivalent), under which the undetected fraction remains substantial at approximately 64% (95% CrI: 57-70%). This is compounded during active epidemic growth, when recently exposed (and therefore pre-symptomatic) cases are overrepresented among travellers. Syndromic airport screening offers limited protection against BVD spread via air travel, and should be complemented by outbreak control at source, strengthened clinical surveillance in receiving countries with high travel connectivity to affected areas, and post-departure self-monitoring guidance for travellers from affected regions.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to estimate the effectiveness of syndromic screening at airports for Bundibugyo ebolavirus disease (BVD) to address the public health challenge of preventing its spread via international air travel.
Design and population
The research used a stochastic simulation model to evaluate exit and/or entry syndromic screening for BVD on a 12-hour international flight from DRC or Uganda.
Core methods
The study employed a stochastic simulation model to estimate screening effectiveness, incorporating parameters such as incubation period (approximately 7.7 days) and onset-to-severe-disease interval (approximately 4 days), and analyzed scenarios with varying proportions of febrile cases.
Main results
At 86% screening sensitivity, approximately 73% of infected travelers would evade combined exit and entry screening (95% CrI: 68-77%), and among those who boarded, approximately 92% would arrive undetected (95% CrI: 90-94%). Even in a conservative scenario where all confirmed cases are febrile, the undetected fraction remained substantial at approximately 64% (95% CrI: 57-70%).
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted the high undetected fraction as reflecting the relative durations of the incubation period and onset-to-severe-disease interval, noting that most infected travelers board before symptom onset, while symptomatic individuals progress too rapidly to travel. The novelty lies in quantifying the limitations of syndromic screening for BVD under realistic epidemic conditions.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest that syndromic airport screening offers limited protection against BVD spread via air travel and should be complemented by outbreak control at source, strengthened clinical surveillance in high-connectivity receiving countries, and post-departure self-monitoring guidance for travelers from affected regions.
Limitations and evidence strength
The evidence strength is moderate, as the study relies on a simulation model with assumptions about incubation periods and symptom presentation, and the proportion of confirmed cases without fever remains uncertain, which could affect the generalizability of the results.

“再次责怪蝙蝠”:YouTube 对 2026 年埃博拉突发公共卫生事件的反应揭示了不信任、错误信息和地缘政治焦虑

"Blame the bats again": YouTube reactions to the 2026 Ebola public health emergency reveal mistrust, misinformation, and geopolitical anxiety
作者： Kaweesi Calvin Nantalaga, Alice Nantege, Nantalaga Calvin Kaweesi, NANTALAGA, K. C.
摘要中文翻译摘要 世界卫生组织宣布2026年暴发刚果民主共和国和乌干达由本迪布焦病毒引起的埃博拉疫情为国际关注的突发公共卫生事件。YouTube 等视频共享平台上的公众参与可以深入了解公众在此类危机期间的看法,特别是在受影响地区,但这些反应在很大程度上仍然没有特征。我们分析了围绕 暴发 的公众言论和情绪,重点关注 YouTube 评论中的主题趋势,使用主题内容分析与主题建模相结合的定性综合。视频是通过 YouTube 数据应用程序编程接口识别的,该接口针对搜索词“埃博拉”和“埃博拉病毒病”各返回 50 个视频。在删除重复项并排除 2026 年 5 月 17 日紧急声明之前发布的视频后,观看次数最多的 10 个视频被保留。从 4,457 条提取的评论中,使用潜在狄利克雷分配主题建模分析了 4,087 条评论,并辅以基于词典的情感分析。出现了五个主题:地缘政治和文化背景;公众人物、阴谋论和错误信息;疾病传播和传播;公共卫生措施和准备;以及宗教和精神的解释。边界担忧、对机构的不信任、全球援助政治以及与 COVID-19 的比较强烈影响了言论,恐惧和信任之间的情绪几乎平分秋色。因此,在此 暴发 期间有效的健康沟通不仅必须解决紧急情况的科学和医学层面,还必须解决其地缘政治、文化和宗教层面,同时反击错误信息并建立公众信任。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在通过分析 YouTube 言论,了解公众对刚果民主共和国和乌干达宣布为国际关注的突发公共卫生事件的 2026 年埃博拉病毒 暴发 的看法和反应。
设计与对象
这项定性研究分析了 YouTube 上的公共言论,重点关注 2026 年 5 月 17 日紧急声明后发布的与“埃博拉”和“埃博拉病毒病”相关的 10 个观看次数最多的视频,从 4,457 条评论中提取了 4,087 条评论。
核心方法
研究人员采用了将主题内容分析与潜在狄利克雷分配主题建模相结合的定性综合,并辅以基于词典的 YouTube 评论情感分析。
主要结果
出现了五个主题趋势:地缘政治和文化背景;公众人物、阴谋论和错误信息;疾病传播和传播;公共卫生措施和准备;以及宗教和精神解释,恐惧和信任之间的情绪几乎平分秋色。
解释与创新
作者将这些发现解释为揭示了地缘政治和文化对公共话语的强烈影响,包括边境问题、对机构的不信任、全球援助政治以及与 COVID-19 的比较,突显了健康危机中错误信息和地缘政治焦虑的新交叉。
科研与公卫意义
埃博拉 暴发 期间有效的健康沟通必须解决科学、医学、地缘政治、文化和宗教层面的问题,同时反击错误信息并建立公众信任,以确保成功的公共卫生应对措施。
局限与证据强度
该研究的证据强度中等,因为它依赖于社交媒体评论的定性分析,尽管系统方法为危机沟通动态提供了有价值的见解,但社交媒体评论可能无法代表更广泛的公众舆论,并且会受到特定平台的偏见的影响。

"Blame the bats again": YouTube reactions to the 2026 Ebola public health emergency reveal mistrust, misinformation, and geopolitical anxiety

Authors: Kaweesi Calvin Nantalaga, Alice Nantege, Nantalaga Calvin Kaweesi, NANTALAGA, K. C.
Original abstract (English)
Abstract The World Health Organization declared the 2026 outbreak of Ebola disease caused by Bundibugyo virus in the Democratic Republic of the Congo and Uganda a Public Health Emergency of International Concern. Public engagement on video-sharing platforms such as YouTube offers insight into public perceptions during such crises, particularly in the affected region, yet these reactions remain largely uncharacterised. We analysed public discourse and sentiment surrounding the outbreak, focusing on thematic trends in YouTube comments, using a qualitative synthesis that combined thematic content analysis with topic modelling. Videos were identified through the YouTube Data application programming interface, which returned 50 videos each for the search terms “Ebola” and “Ebola virus disease.” After removing duplicates and excluding videos published before the 17 May 2026 emergency declaration, the 10 most-viewed videos were retained. From 4,457 extracted comments, 4,087 were analysed using Latent Dirichlet Allocation topic modelling, complemented by a lexicon-based sentiment analysis. Five themes emerged: geopolitical and cultural context; public figures, conspiracy theories, and misinformation; disease spread and transmission; public health measures and preparedness; and religious and spiritual interpretation. Discourse was strongly shaped by border concerns, mistrust of institutions, global aid politics, and comparisons with COVID-19, and sentiment was split near-evenly between fear and trust. Effective health communication during this outbreak must therefore address not only the scientific and medical dimensions of the emergency but also its geopolitical, cultural, and religious dimensions, while countering misinformation and building public trust.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to characterize public perceptions and reactions to the 2026 Ebola outbreak caused by Bundibugyo virus in the Democratic Republic of the Congo and Uganda, declared a Public Health Emergency of International Concern, by analyzing YouTube discourse.
Design and population
This qualitative study analyzed public discourse on YouTube, focusing on the 10 most-viewed videos related to 'Ebola' and 'Ebola virus disease' published after the 17 May 2026 emergency declaration, with 4,087 comments extracted from 4,457 total comments.
Core methods
The researchers employed a qualitative synthesis combining thematic content analysis with Latent Dirichlet Allocation topic modelling, complemented by a lexicon-based sentiment analysis of YouTube comments.
Main results
Five thematic trends emerged: geopolitical and cultural context; public figures, conspiracy theories, and misinformation; disease spread and transmission; public health measures and preparedness; and religious and spiritual interpretation, with sentiment split nearly evenly between fear and trust.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted the findings as revealing strong geopolitical and cultural influences on public discourse, including border concerns, mistrust of institutions, global aid politics, and comparisons with COVID-19, highlighting the novel intersection of misinformation and geopolitical anxiety in health crises.
Scientific and public-health significance
Effective health communication during Ebola outbreaks must address scientific, medical, geopolitical, cultural, and religious dimensions while countering misinformation and building public trust to ensure successful public health responses.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study's evidence strength is moderate due to its reliance on qualitative analysis of social media comments, which may not represent broader public opinion and is subject to platform-specific biases, though the systematic approach provides valuable insights into crisis communication dynamics.

Journal: The Journal of Immunology  |  Canonical publication date: 2026-07-28  |  DOI: 10.1093/jimmun/vkag141.1350  |  Date basis: online_date  |  Publication status: Current window  |  Citation: 215, Supplement_1
C 补充信息学术文献 · 研究C supplementaryAcademic literature · Research

发烧温度会根据亲和力成熟程度不同地改变埃博拉抗体功能 2306819

Fever Temperatures Differentially Alter Ebola-Antibody Functionality Depending on Affinity Maturation 2306819
作者： Lianna Wood, Taras Chicz, Ross Blanc, Lindsay McManus, Qixin Wang, Harry Bertera, César Muñoz-Fontela, Beartriz Escudero Perez, Ryan McNamara
摘要中文翻译摘要 介绍 2013-16 年,西非经历了已知最大规模的高致命病毒埃博拉病毒(EBOV)暴发,此后又发生了持续的散发性 暴发。埃博拉病毒是埃博拉病毒出血热的病原体,其特征是高烧。对 EBOV 的抗体反应与预防 EBOV 疾病相关。然而,尚不清楚抗体的功能如何防止重复暴露。了解抗体介导的埃博拉感染保护机制对于最大限度地长期预防感染至关重要。方法 我们利用系统 血清学 方法对 2013-16 几内亚埃博拉疫情的 60 名幸存者和刚果共和国的 81 名侏儒血统个体进行 队列研究 分析,这些幸存者没有记录到埃博拉病毒感染,但确实经历过频繁的人畜共患病暴露,以表征发烧状况对亲和力成熟的埃博拉病毒导向抗体反应的影响。在生理温度 (37°C) 和高烧温度 (40°C) 下测量抗体结合和细胞激活。结果我们发现,对于亲和力成熟的抗体反应和非特异性抗体反应,在发烧温度下,EBOV 的中和作用都会受到损害。相比之下,效应子介导的功能,特别是针对 EBOV 可溶性糖蛋白(sGP,以前与动物 疫苗 攻击模型中增强保护有关的特征),在发热时会增强。这种效应功能的温度敏感性增强与全长 GP 和 sGP 导向的 IgG 亚类、IgA、FcγR 结合抗体和 FcαR 结合抗体相关,证明了在发热期间维持的高度相关的体液特征网络。结论 总的来说,我们的研究结果表明,虽然中和在非发烧状态下的 监测 中发挥关键作用,但发烧调节亲和力成熟的抗体对引起出血热的 EBOV 的反应,将抗体谱转变为前效应子功能表型。资金来源 NIH NIAID U19 AI135995 主题类别 病毒免疫学 (VIR)
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
这项研究需要了解抗体介导的针对 埃博拉病毒 感染的保护是如何发挥作用的,特别是发烧情况如何影响抗体功能,因为这些知识对于最大限度地提高长期感染保护至关重要。
设计与对象
该研究利用系统 血清学 方法对 2013-16 年几内亚埃博拉疫情的 60 名幸存者和刚果共和国的 81 名侏儒血统个体进行了 队列研究 分析,这些幸存者没有记录到埃博拉病毒感染情况,但经常接触人畜共患病。
核心方法
在生理 (37°C) 和高烧 (40°C) 温度下测量抗体结合和细胞激活,以表征发烧条件对亲和力成熟的 EBOV 导向抗体反应的影响。
主要结果
研究表明,在发烧温度下,亲和力成熟和非特异性抗体反应的埃博拉病毒中和作用都会受到损害,而效应介导的功能,特别是针对埃博拉病毒可溶性糖蛋白的功能,在发烧温度下会增强。
解释与创新
作者解释说,发烧调节亲和力成熟的抗体对埃博拉病毒的反应,将抗体谱转变为前效应子功能表型,这是理解温度依赖性抗体功能的一个新发现。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果为 监测 和 疫苗 的设计提供了信息,表明虽然中和作用在非发烧状态下是关键,但发烧条件增强了效应器功能,可用于改进保护策略。
局限与证据强度
由于该研究的观察性质和对仅抽象数据的依赖,可能缺乏详细的方法和统计严谨性,因此证据强度中等。

Fever Temperatures Differentially Alter Ebola-Antibody Functionality Depending on Affinity Maturation 2306819

Authors: Lianna Wood, Taras Chicz, Ross Blanc, Lindsay McManus, Qixin Wang, Harry Bertera, César Muñoz-Fontela, Beartriz Escudero Perez, Ryan McNamara
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Introduction In 2013-16, Western Africa experienced the largest known outbreak of the highly lethal virus, Ebolavirus (EBOV) and ongoing sporadic outbreaks have occurred since. EBOV is the causative agent of EBOV hemorrhagic fever, characterized by a high-grade fever. Antibody responses to EBOV have been correlated with protection against EBOV disease. However, it is unclear how antibody functions result in protection from repeat exposure. Understanding antibody-mediated mechanisms of protection against Ebola infection is critical to maximize long-term protection against infection. Methods We utilize a systems serology approach to analyze a cohort of 60 survivors of the 2013-16 Ebola epidemic in Guinea and 81 individuals of pygmy ancestry in the Republic of Congo who did not have documented EBOV infection, but do experience frequent zoonotic exposures to characterize the impact of fever conditions on affinity matured EBOV-directed antibody responses. Antibody binding and cellular activation were measured at both physiologic (37C) and high grade fever (40C) temperatures. Results We show that neutralization of EBOV is impaired under fever temperatures, both for affinity-matured and non-specific antibody responses. In contrast, effector-mediated functions, particularly against the EBOV soluble glycoprotein (sGP, a signature previously linked to enhanced protection in animal vaccine challenge models), are enhanced at febrile temperatures. This temperature-sensitive enhancement of effector function correlates with full length GP and sGP-directed IgG subclasses, IgA, FcγR-binding- and FcαR-binding antibodies, demonstrating a highly associated network of humoral features that are maintained during periods of febrile temperatures. Conclusion Collectively, our findings suggest that although neutralization plays a key role in surveillance in a non-fever state, fever modulates affinity-matured antibody responses to the hemorrhagic fever-inducing EBOV, moving antibody profiles to a pro-effector function phenotype. Funding Source NIH NIAID U19 AI135995 Topic Categories Viral Immunology (VIR)
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study was needed to understand how antibody-mediated protection against Ebola virus infection works, particularly how fever conditions affect antibody functionality, as this knowledge is critical for maximizing long-term protection against infection.
Design and population
The study utilized a systems serology approach to analyze a cohort of 60 survivors of the 2013-16 Ebola epidemic in Guinea and 81 individuals of pygmy ancestry in the Republic of Congo who did not have documented EBOV infection but experience frequent zoonotic exposures.
Core methods
Antibody binding and cellular activation were measured at both physiologic (37°C) and high-grade fever (40°C) temperatures to characterize the impact of fever conditions on affinity-matured EBOV-directed antibody responses.
Main results
The study showed that neutralization of EBOV is impaired under fever temperatures for both affinity-matured and non-specific antibody responses, while effector-mediated functions, particularly against the EBOV soluble glycoprotein, are enhanced at febrile temperatures.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted that fever modulates affinity-matured antibody responses to EBOV, shifting antibody profiles to a pro-effector function phenotype, which is a novel finding in understanding temperature-dependent antibody functionality.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings inform surveillance and vaccine design by suggesting that while neutralization is key in non-fever states, fever conditions enhance effector functions, which could be leveraged for improved protection strategies.
Limitations and evidence strength
The evidence strength is moderate due to the observational nature of the study and reliance on abstract-only data, which may lack detailed methodological and statistical rigor.

It’s not just virology: The Bundibugyo Ebola outbreak in the Democratic Republic of the Congo and Uganda demands a wider public health response
作者： Anne Baber Wallis, Friday Okonofua
摘要中文翻译当前刚果民主共和国(DRC)和乌干达由本迪布焦病毒(本迪布焦正埃博拉病毒属)引起的埃博拉疫情不仅仅是另一种传染病紧急情况。它提醒我们,流行病不仅来自生物病原体,还来自社会不平等、环境破坏、脆弱的卫生系统和持续的武装冲突。虽然埃博拉病毒因其令人恐惧的症状和高 死亡率 而成为头条新闻,但导致其传播的条件却很少受到关注。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究解决了对本迪布焦埃博拉 暴发 采取更广泛的公共卫生应对措施的需要,强调了该疾病传播中生物因素和社会因素之间复杂的相互作用。
设计与对象
该研究调查了刚果民主共和国和乌干达的本迪布焦病毒 暴发,重点关注导致病毒传播的社会和环境条件。
核心方法
这些方法涉及分析 暴发 的背景,包括社会不平等、环境破坏以及受影响地区的卫生系统状况。
主要结果
暴发 表明,本迪布焦病毒传播的条件深深植根于社会和环境因素,而不仅仅是生物因素。
解释与创新
作者将这些发现解释为呼吁解决 暴发 的潜在社会和环境决定因素,强调需要采取多方面的公共卫生方法。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明将社会和环境因素纳入预防和管理埃博拉病毒 暴发 的公共卫生战略的重要性。
局限与证据强度
该研究仅限于抽象层面的分析,没有提供详细的统计或方法数据,导致置信度评估适中。

It’s not just virology: The Bundibugyo Ebola outbreak in the Democratic Republic of the Congo and Uganda demands a wider public health response

Authors: Anne Baber Wallis, Friday Okonofua
Original abstract (English)
The current outbreak of Ebola disease caused by the Bundibugyo virus (species Orthoebolavirus bundibugyoense) in the Democratic Republic of the Congo (DRC) and Uganda is much more than another infectious disease emergency. It reminds us that epidemics come not only from biological pathogens but also from social inequities, environmental disruption, fragile health systems, and persistent armed conflict. While Ebola captures headlines because of its fear-provoking symptoms and high mortality, the conditions that allow it to spread receive far less attention.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study addresses the need for a broader public health response to the Bundibugyo Ebola outbreak, highlighting the complex interplay between biological and social factors in the spread of the disease.
Design and population
The study examines the Bundibugyo virus outbreak in the Democratic Republic of the Congo and Uganda, focusing on the social and environmental conditions that contribute to the spread of the virus.
Core methods
The methods involve analyzing the outbreak's context, including social inequities, environmental disruption, and the state of health systems in the affected regions.
Main results
The outbreak demonstrates that the conditions enabling the spread of the Bundibugyo virus are deeply rooted in social and environmental factors rather than solely biological ones.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpret the findings as a call to address the underlying social and environmental determinants of the outbreak, emphasizing the need for a multifaceted public health approach.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings inform the importance of integrating social and environmental factors into public health strategies for preventing and managing Ebola outbreaks.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited to an abstract-level analysis and does not provide detailed statistical or methodological data, resulting in a moderate confidence assessment.

C 补充信息学术文献 · 综述C supplementaryAcademic literature · Review

刚果民主共和国和乌干达的本迪布焦埃博拉 暴发 揭示了西非的流行病防范工作

What the Bundibugyo Ebola outbreak in the Democratic Republic of the Congo and Uganda reveals about epidemic preparedness in West Africa
作者： Virgil Kuassi Lokossou, Aishat Bukola Usman, Oyeronke Oyebanj, Kehinde Ogunyemi, Adama N’Dir, Issiaka Sombie, Melchior Athanase Aïssi, Adama Ndir, Melchior Athanase Aissi
摘要中文翻译2026年刚果民主共和国(DRC)和乌干达的邦迪布焦埃博拉病毒暴发暴露了疫情准备方面的关键脆弱性,尽管在前几次埃博拉疫情后已对卫生安全进行了大量投资。与由埃博拉病毒埃博拉病毒的扎伊尔株引起的暴发不同,目前尚无针对邦迪布焦埃博拉病毒埃博拉病毒病的许可疫苗、获批的治疗药物或广泛可用的快速诊断方法。因此,受影响国家不得不在同时生成指导控制措施的循证证据的同时应对活跃的暴发。在本观点文章中,我们认为,尽管存在大量全球框架,包括CEPI的100天任务、世卫组织的研发蓝图和非洲疾控中心的倡议,但在区域和国家层面实施研究准备系统仍是需要持续投入的领域。借鉴2014-2016年埃博拉疫情后西非的经验,我们假设,预先批准的方案、常设伦理机制、临床试验网络和紧急融资安排的缺失可能比医疗对策本身的缺失代表更关键的差距。我们希望澄清,我们的论点并非认为在暴发期间研究缺失;事实上,在邦迪布焦埃博拉病毒暴发期间已开展了大量研究工作,而是研究启动的速度因预先部署系统的缺失而受到限制。我们反思了监测、实验室系统和区域协调方面的进展,同时强调了在监管协调、紧急融资、跨境合作和社区参与方面存在的持续弱点。我们讨论了西非的拉沙热联盟和全球性倡议(如CEPI的100天任务)如何为危机前加强准备工作提供了有用的框架。我们认为,疫情准备不仅应被视为技术性工作,还应被视为政治、制度和社会投资,而研究准备需要作为卫生安全的核心组成部分得到系统性关注。如果西非要有效应对未来的疫情威胁,对研究准备、融资和区域合作的持续承诺将是必不可少的。
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
该审查审查了刚果民主共和国和乌干达的本迪布焦埃博拉暴发,以评估西非的流行病准备情况,重点关注尽管先前进行了投资,但卫生安全方面的脆弱性。
证据基础与方法
该评论是一篇透视文章,借鉴了 2014-2016 年西非埃博拉疫情和 2026 年 Bundibugyo 暴发 的经验,没有具体说明数据库、搜索日期或研究计数。
共识与结论
实质性的全球框架已经存在,但区域和国家层面的研究准备工作的实施需要持续的投资;缺乏预先批准的方案、道德机制、临床试验网络和紧急融资是一个关键差距。
争议与缺口
研究工作在 暴发 期间进行,但由于缺乏预先定位的系统,速度受到限制;持续存在的弱点包括监管协调、紧急融资、跨境合作和社区参与。
科研与实践启示
流行病防范应被视为一项政治、制度和社会投资;对研究准备、融资和区域合作的持续承诺对于有效应对未来威胁至关重要。

What the Bundibugyo Ebola outbreak in the Democratic Republic of the Congo and Uganda reveals about epidemic preparedness in West Africa

Authors: Virgil Kuassi Lokossou, Aishat Bukola Usman, Oyeronke Oyebanj, Kehinde Ogunyemi, Adama N’Dir, Issiaka Sombie, Melchior Athanase Aïssi, Adama Ndir, Melchior Athanase Aissi
Original abstract (English)
The 2026 Bundibugyo Ebola outbreak in the Democratic Republic of the Congo (DRC) and Uganda has exposed critical vulnerabilities in epidemic preparedness despite substantial investments in health security following previous Ebola epidemics. Unlike outbreaks caused by the Zaire strain of Ebola virus, no licensed vaccines, approved therapeutics, or widely available rapid diagnostics currently exist for Bundibugyo Ebola virus disease. Consequently, affected countries have had to respond to an active outbreak while simultaneously generating evidence to guide control measures. In this perspective, we argue that while substantial global frameworks exist, including CEPI’s 100 Days Mission, WHO’s R&D Blueprint, and Africa CDC’s initiatives, the operationalization of research readiness systems at regional and national levels remains an area requiring continued investment. Drawing on experiences from West Africa following the 2014–2016 Ebola epidemic, we hypothesize that the absence of pre-approved protocols, standing ethics mechanisms, clinical trial networks, and emergency financing arrangements may represent a more critical gap than the absence of medical countermeasures themselves. We wish to clarify that our argument is not that research is absent during outbreaks; indeed, significant research efforts have been mounted during the Bundibugyo outbreak, but rather that the speed with which research can be initiated is constrained by the absence of pre-positioned systems. We reflect on progress in surveillance, laboratory systems, and regional coordination while highlighting persistent weaknesses in regulatory harmonization, emergency financing, cross-border collaboration, and community engagement. We discuss how the Lassa Fever Coalition in West Africa and global initiatives such as CEPI’s 100 Days Mission provide useful frameworks for strengthening preparedness before crises occur. We contend that epidemic preparedness should be viewed not only as a technical exercise but also as a political, institutional, and social investment and that research readiness requires systematic attention as a core component of health security. Sustained commitment to research readiness, financing, and regional collaboration will be essential if West Africa is to respond effectively to future epidemic threats.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
The review examines the Bundibugyo Ebola outbreak in the Democratic Republic of the Congo and Uganda to assess epidemic preparedness in West Africa, focusing on vulnerabilities in health security despite prior investments.
Evidence base and method
The review is a perspective article drawing on experiences from the 2014–2016 Ebola epidemic in West Africa and the 2026 Bundibugyo outbreak, without specifying databases, search dates, or study counts.
Consensus and conclusions
Substantial global frameworks exist, but operationalization of research readiness at regional and national levels requires continued investment; the absence of pre-approved protocols, ethics mechanisms, clinical trial networks, and emergency financing is a critical gap.
Controversies and gaps
Research efforts occur during outbreaks, but speed is constrained by lack of pre-positioned systems; persistent weaknesses include regulatory harmonization, emergency financing, cross-border collaboration, and community engagement.
Research and practice implications
Epidemic preparedness should be viewed as a political, institutional, and social investment; sustained commitment to research readiness, financing, and regional collaboration is essential for effective response to future threats.

埃博拉本迪布焦病毒病 暴发:刚果民主共和国 |乌干达每周外部形势报告 10,数据截至 2026 年 7 月 19 日 - ReliefWeb

Ebola Bundibugyo virus disease outbreak: Democratic Republic of the Congo | Uganda Weekly External Situation Report 10, Data as of 19 July 2026 - ReliefWeb
新闻简报The Democratic Republic of the Congo is experiencing a deteriorating outbreak of Bundibugyo virus disease, with intense transmission and geographic expansion. Between 24 June and 19 July 2026, 460 new confirmed cases and 248 new deaths were reported, bringing the cumulative total to 2,423 cases and 967 deaths (CFR: 39.9%). Ituri Province remains the epicenter, accounting for 89.1% of cases. Transmission is highly concentrated in 10 health zones, which collectively represent 86.8% of cases. Despite geographic expansion to 47 health zones, seven zones have reported no cases for over 21 days. Challenges include delayed case detection and high mortality outside treatment centers (59.4% of deaths). The risk of cross-border spread persists, necessitating sustained regional surveillance and response efforts. No new cases were reported in neighboring Uganda or France.
查看新闻五要素
时间
发布日期:2026年7月28日。活动期间:数据截至2026年7月19日,报告涵盖2026年6月24日至7月19日期间,最近一周为2026年7月13日至19日。
地点与对象
刚果民主共和国,特别是伊图里省(震中)、北基伍省、南基伍省和上韦莱省。受影响人群包括 47 个卫生区的居民,其中布尼亚、尼兹、鲁万帕拉、蒙布瓦卢和卡特瓦的 传播 最为著名。尼兹的境内流离失所者 (IDP) 营地被列为弱势群体。
事件
刚果民主共和国确认存在本迪布焦病毒病 (BVD) 暴发,传播 持续强烈、地域扩张、病例数和死亡人数增加。自上次更新以来,情况正在恶化,新增确诊病例 460 例,新增确诊死亡 248 例。乌干达或法国没有报告新病例或继发性 传播 病例。
规模、影响与风险
累计确诊病例:2,423例;确诊死亡人数:967;粗病死率(CFR):39.9%。伊图里省占病例数的 89.1%,死亡数的 83.9%。传播高度集中,10个卫生区占病例数的86.8%和死亡数的88.9%。由于传播的地域扩张和持续存在,跨境传播的风险仍然很高。
应对、状态与不确定性
调查和应对工作仍在进行中,面临的挑战包括病例发现延迟、就诊延迟以及指定 治疗 中心外死亡比例较高(总体为 59.4%,最近一周为 65.4%)。七个卫生区已经超过 21 天没有确诊病例,这表明如果 监测 敏感,则最近的 传播 可能不存在。强调区域 监测、准备和响应工作,以减轻跨境传播。

Ebola Bundibugyo virus disease outbreak: Democratic Republic of the Congo | Uganda Weekly External Situation Report 10, Data as of 19 July 2026 - ReliefWeb

News Brief
The Democratic Republic of the Congo is experiencing a deteriorating outbreak of Bundibugyo virus disease, with intense transmission and geographic expansion. Between 24 June and 19 July 2026, 460 new confirmed cases and 248 new deaths were reported, bringing the cumulative total to 2,423 cases and 967 deaths (CFR: 39.9%). Ituri Province remains the epicenter, accounting for 89.1% of cases. Transmission is highly concentrated in 10 health zones, which collectively represent 86.8% of cases. Despite geographic expansion to 47 health zones, seven zones have reported no cases for over 21 days. Challenges include delayed case detection and high mortality outside treatment centers (59.4% of deaths). The risk of cross-border spread persists, necessitating sustained regional surveillance and response efforts. No new cases were reported in neighboring Uganda or France.
